The options are 'DUKPT2009'  which represents the Derived Unique Key Per Transaction (DUKPT) algorithm, as defined by  ANSI X9.24-2009 Annex A, and 'AESDUKPT128ECB', which signifies the AES DUKPT ECB algorithm with a  key length of 128 bits, as defined in ANSI X9.24-3-2017 Annex.\n2. Mac Algo: This points to the algorithm used for Message Authentication Code (MAC). There are two  options: 'RetailSHA256MAC' which indicates the Retail-CBC-MAC using SHA-256 (Secure Hash standard)  and an ASN.1 Object Identifier: id-retail-cbc-mac-sha-256. The other option is 'SHA256CMACwithAES128',  which represents the CMAC (Cipher-based Message Authentication Code) as defined by NIST 800-38B -  May 2005. This option employs the Advanced Encryption Standard block cipher with a 128-bit  cryptographic key, as approved by FIPS 197 - November 6, 2001. Optional for Subsequent Payment or Subsequent Split Shipment."},"xSrcCxFlowId":{"description":"The X-Src-Cx-Flow-Id helps in routing and session affinity. Usage of X-Src-Cx-Flow-Id in requests ensures requests by the same client are directed to the same server. When you make your initial API call, use the X-Src-Cx-Flow-Id, returned in one of the client-side calls checkoutWithNewCard() or checkoutWitCard(). If you do not send X-Src-Cx-Flow-Id in your request or if the field is empty, the Click to Pay system generates one and responds with the X-Src-Cx-Flow-Id in the header. The X-Src-Cx-Flow-Id returned in the response can be used in the subsequent calls to ensure these calls are directed to the same server that returned the initial response.","type":"string"},"organizationalId":{"type":"string","description":"Open API key to make the server to server call to Mastercard SRCI. This ID is provided to the client during the onboarding process to make OAuth1.0 based signed requests. information from the card, such as track1/2/3, card number, expiry date, card code, and/or the name of the cardholder. The data is encrypted and then transformed into a Base64-encoded string. Specifically, the plaintext card data is converted into a UTF-8 JSON block, padded according to the Padded80 standard, and then encrypted. The resultant encrypted data is encoded using Base64. The formula for this transformation is: base64(encrypt(Padded80(UTF-8(json-block.plainCardData)))).\n"},"key":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Key"}}}]},"GetEligibleInstallmentPlansResponse":{"description":"Response for Get Eligible Only needed in case of offline verification was performed.","type":"string","enum":["NO_VERIFICATION","OFFLINE_PIN","PAPER_SIGNATURE","DIGITAL_SIGNATURE","CARD_CODE_VALIDATION","ADDRESS_VALIDATION"],"example":"SIGNATURE"},"cardholderPresentIndicator":{"description":"Indicates whether the cardholder is physically present or not present during the transaction.","type":"boolean","default":true,"example":true},"cardPresentIndicator":{"description":"Specifies if the actual payment card is present at the time of the transaction. This is typically used to differentiate between card-present and card-not-present transactions.","type":"boolean","default":true,"example":true},"singleTapPinPerformed":{"description":"Indicates if single Tap Pin was performed after scheme response.","type":"boolean","default":false,"example":false},"offlineIndicator":{"description":"Specifies if the transaction was processed offline. Useful for situations where network connectivity is intermittent or unavailable.","type":"boolean","default":false,"example":false},"onlineReason":{"type":"string","enum":["RANDOM_SELECTION","ICC_FORCED","MERCHANT_FORCED","TERMINAL_FORCED","ISSUER_FORCED","FLOOR_LIMIT","EXCEPTION_FILE","TOTAL_AMOUNT","CONTROLLED_BIN","UNKNOWN_BIN","CONTROLLED_PAN","FLOW_CONTROL","UNAVAILABLE_CURRENCY"],"description":"The `onlineReason` field within the `terminalRequestData` object specifies the reason why a card-present transaction is being processed online. The values of this field assist the processing system in understanding why the transaction is not occurring offline (i.e., directly at the terminal), enabling more accurate transaction processing and risk assessment. Possible values include:\n  - `RANDOM_SELECTION`: Transaction is randomly selected to go online.\n  - `ICC_FORCED`: Payment application in the Integrated Circuit Card (ICC) forces the transaction to go online.\n  - `MERCHANT_FORCED`: Online processing is forced by the card acceptor.\n  - `TERMINAL_FORCED`: Terminal randomly selects the transaction to go online.\n  - `ISSUER_FORCED`: Online processing is forced by the card issuer.\n  - `FLOOR_LIMIT`: Transaction exceeds the floor limit.\n  - `EXCEPTION_FILE`: Card appears on terminal exception file.\n  - `TOTAL_AMOUNT`: The total amount of purchases per cardholder and per application exceeds the floor limit.\n  - `CONTROLLED_BIN`: The Bank Identification Number (BIN) is under control.\n  - `UNKNOWN_BIN`: The Bank Identification Number (BIN) is unknown.\n  - `CONTROLLED_PAN`: The Primary Account Number (PAN or card number) is under control.\n  - `FLOW_CONTROL`: The reason relates to flow control.\n  - `UNAVAILABLE_CURRENCY`: The currency is unknown or unavailable.\n","example":"ICC_FORCED"},"attendanceContext":{"type":"string","enum":["ATTENDED","UNATTENDED_LEVEL_1","UNATTENDED_LEVEL_2"],"description":"Specifies the attendance condition during the transaction - ATTENDED if the transaction was supervised by a staff member, UNATTENDED_LEVEL_1 for transactions via automated dispensing machines or other devices with level 1 security (PIN required), and UNATTENDED_LEVEL_2 for transactions at self-service terminals with level 2 security.\n","default":"ATTENDED","example":"ATTENDED"},"emvData":{"description":"Electronic Verification Method data. This field is mandatory if 'posEntryMode' is either 'contactless' or 'contact'.","type":"string","maxLength":10000},"approvalCode":{"description":"Offline transaction approvalcode.","type":"string","maxLength":6},"traceNumber":{"description":"Transaction counter.","type":"integer","format":"int32","minimum":0,"maximum":999999},"pin":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Pin"},"emergencyProcessing":{"description":"Support emergency processing","type":"boolean"}},"required":["posEntryMode","offlineVerificationPerformed"]},"PointOfSaleDevice":{"description":"Terminal Request Data for card present transactions.","type":"object","properties":{"terminalProvider":{"type":"string","description":"The vendor that provided the terminal to merchant.","example":"VENDOR"},"model":{"type":"string","description":"The specific model or version of the POS device or terminal.","example":"A50"},"serialNumber":{"type":"string","description":"The unique identifier assigned by the manufacturer to the POS device or terminal.","example":"edfd-193ad"},"softwareProvider":{"type":"string","description":"The entity that provides the software running on the POS device or terminal.","example":"Vendor"},"softwareVersion":{"type":"string","description":"The version number of the software installed on the POS device or terminal.","example":"1.0.1"},"certificationNumber":{"type":"string","minLength":10,"maxLength":10,"pattern":"^[0-9]{10}$","description":"Certification number assigned by an official entity. Required for girocard transactions; maps to the terminal approval number sent to ZVT/Poseidon in BMP61 field 11.","example":"0202000003"},"capabilities":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Capabilities"}},"required":["terminalProvider","model","serialNumber"]},"Capabilities":{"description":"Capabilities of used device to read card data and verify cardholder.","type":"object","properties":{"cardholderVerificationCapabilities":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","enum":["NO_VERIFICATION","OFFLINE_PIN","ONLINE_PIN","PAPER_SIGNATURE","DIGITAL_SIGNATURE","CARD_CODE_VALIDATION","ADDRESS_VALIDATION"]},"description":"Specifies the different capabilities the point-of-sale (POS) device or terminal has for verifying a cardholder's authenticity. These capabilities include:\n- OFFLINE_PIN: Verification using a PIN without needing an online connection.\n- ONLINE_PIN: Verification using a PIN that requires an online check with the issuing bank.\n- PAPER_SIGNATURE: Verification through the cardholder's handwritten signature on a physical paper receipt.\n- DIGITAL_SIGNATURE: Verification through a digital rendition of the cardholder's signature.\n- NO_VERIFICATION: The device does not have any specific cardholder verification capability.\n- CARD_CODE_VALIDATION: Verification using card security code. \n- ADDRESS_VALIDATION: Verification using address data like street address and postal code.\n"},"cardReadingCapabilities":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","enum":["MANUAL","MAGNETIC_STRIPE","MAGNETIC_STRIPE_FALLBACK","MAGNETIC_STRIPE_INCOMPLETE","CONTACT_EMV","CONTACTLESS_EMV","CONTACTLESS_MAGSTRIPE","DATA_ON_FILE","BARCODE","TAG"]},"description":"Indicates the various methods by which the device can read card information:\n- MANUAL: Card information is manually entered by the merchant, often used when the physical card is not present or the other reading methods fail.\n- MAGNETIC_STRIPE: Reads information from the magnetic stripe found on the back of many payment cards.\n- MAGNETIC_STRIPE_FALLBACK: Used when the primary magnetic stripe reading fails and a secondary attempt is made to read.\n- MAGNETIC_STRIPE_INCOMPLETE: When card data is not read correctly at the POS device.\n- CONTACT_EMV: Direct physical contact is required between the card and the reader (ICC).\n- CONTACTLESS_EMV: Allows for card reading without physical contact, usually via RFID or NFC technologies.\n- CONTACTLESS_MAGSTRIPE: Combines contactless reading with the ability to read from a magnetic stripe.\n- DATA_ON_FILE: Uses card details that have been previously stored or recorded, allowing for seamless transactions without needing the card to be read again.\n- BARCODE: Reads card information from a barcode representation.\n- TAG: Uses a tagged identifier for card data retrieval.\n"},"terminalType":{"type":"string","enum":["POS","MPOS","SOFTPOS"],"default":"POS","description":"Indicates the type of the terminal based on its functionalities and usage:\n- POS: Traditional point-of-sale terminal used for processing card payments.\n- MPOS: Mobile point-of-sale terminal that can be used with mobile devices for payment processing.\n- SOFTPOS: A software-based point-of-sale solution that allows a device to accept payments without the need for dedicated hardware.\n","example":"POS"},"pinCapability":{"type":"string","enum":["HARDWARE","SOFTWARE","DISABLED","NONE"],"description":"Describes the capability of the device to accept PIN entry for transactions:\n- HARDWARE: The device can accept PIN entry through dedicated hardware.\n- SOFTWARE: The device can accept PIN entry through a software interface.\n- DISABLED: PIN entry capability is present but currently disabled.\n- NONE: The device does not have the capability to accept PIN entries.\n","example":"SOFTWARE"},"features":{"description":"Additional features that are available on the used POS Device.","type":"array","items":{"type":"string","enum":["SINGLE_TAP_PIN_SUPPORT"]}}},"required":["cardVerificationCapabilities","cardReadingCapabilities","pinCapability"]},"Key":{"type":"object","properties":{"index":{"type":"string","description":"In case of DUKPT, this field contains the KSN. There might be other key derivation algorithms not requiring an index."},"name":{"type":"string","description":"Name of the used key"},"version":{"type":"string","description":"Version of the used key"},"derivationAlgo":{"type":"string","enum":["DUKPT2009","AESDUKPT128ECB","MASTERSESSION"],"description":"Represents the algorithm used for key derivation. Possible values include 'DUKPT2009', which stands for the Derived Unique Key Per Transaction (DUKPT) algorithm as specified in ANSI X9.24-2009 Annex A, and 'AESDUKPT128ECB', representing the AES DUKPT ECB algorithm with a key length of 128 bits as defined in ANSI X9.24-3-2017 Annex. Other possible value is MASTERSESSION used for key exchange in Host-to-host connections.\n"},"encryptionAlgo":{"type":"string","enum":["DES112CBC","AES128CBC","TDES_ECB","AES128_ECB"],"description":"Applied encryption algorithm.  - For PIN TDES_ECB for ISO0 and ISO1 AES128_ECB for ISO4 - For SRED possible values are DES112CBC and AES128CBC\n"},"initializationVector":{"type":"string","description":"Initialization vector in Hex format using in MASTERSESSION derivationAlgo (if not send, we will assume the vector is all zeros). For DUKPT is not required.","example":"EED54DF375E2BA9557EF0636CF8A72E6"}}},"Pin":{"description":"Termianl Request Data for card present transactions.","type":"object","properties":{"value":{"type":"string"},"formatCode":{"type":"string","enum":["ISO0","ISO1","ISO4"],"description":"Format code of the used PIN, present if online PIN was used","example":"ISO0 (for TDES), ISO1 (for TDES), ISO4 (for AES)"},"key":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Key"}}},"PaymentCardProtected":{"description":"This object encapsulates sensitive card data in an encrypted format, ensuring that it's protected  during transmission and storage.\n","type":"object","properties":{"encryptedData":{"type":"string","description":"This field holds the encrypted information from the card, such as track1/2/3, card number, expiry date, card code, and/or the name of the cardholder. The data is encrypted and then transformed into a Base64-encoded string. Specifically, the plaintext card data is converted into a UTF-8 JSON block, padded according to the Padded80 standard, and then encrypted. The resultant encrypted data is encoded using Base64. The formula for this transformation is: base64(encrypt(Padded80(UTF-8(json-block.plainCardData)))).\n"},"key":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/Key"},"cardAccountType":{"$ref":"#/components/schemas/CardAccountType"}}},"PaymentCardWithTrackData":{"description":"This card."},"track3":{"type":"string","maxLength":104,"description":"Contains the third track of data encoded on the magnetic strip of the card, if
